<div class="message-content" style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;color:#333333;max-width:600px;margin:30px auto;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif">

  <p style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif;color:#333333;margin-bottom:1em;margin-top:0;text-align:center;" rel="text-align:center;"><img src="https://embed.filekitcdn.com/e/b6n8ecCqtSrgGUcvA3VyNe/9KMyAmBekzzWtYiWQuQKtF?w=400&amp;fit=max" style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif;color:#333333;max-width:100%;height:auto;width:400px;">
</p>
<p style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif;color:#333333;margin-bottom:1em;margin-top:0">Dear Writer,
</p>
<p style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif;color:#333333;margin-bottom:1em;margin-top:0"><img style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif;color:#333333;max-width:100%;height:auto;border-radius:150px;float:right;margin-bottom:10px;margin-left:10px;width:180px;" src="https://embed.filekitcdn.com/e/b6n8ecCqtSrgGUcvA3VyNe/swTzWQhngX97e1T5yUPkgb?w=180&amp;fit=max">
</p>
<p style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif;color:#333333;margin-bottom:1em;margin-top:0"><span style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif;color:#333333;">I’m Frederick Meyer, the Director here at Writers.com, and I’m thrilled to welcome you to the Writers.com community!</span>
</p>
<p style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif;color:#333333;margin-bottom:1em;margin-top:0">You signed up because you’re serious about writing, right? Well, let’s talk about what it <em style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif;color:#333333">really</em> takes to succeed as a writer. And no, it’s not about talent or degrees.
</p>
<p style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif;color:#333333;margin-bottom:1em;margin-top:0">Let me tell you a secret. Writing success comes down to seven simple things — I call them the <em style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif;color:#333333">7 Cs</em>. Miss any of these, and the journey becomes a lot tougher. But if you have them, you'll be well on your way to fulfilling your writing dreams.
</p>
<p style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif;color:#333333;margin-bottom:1em;margin-top:0">Over the next week, you'll receive 7 emails that will help you become a better writer, step by step.
</p>
<p style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif;color:#333333;margin-bottom:1em;margin-top:0">In the meantime, <a href="https://47e72b72.click.convertkit-mail.com/92uxn79q48idu7xplruzuzdd4d33swh3wok/kkhmh2ul7v3gqptk/aHR0cHM6Ly93cml0ZXJzLmNvbS9kb3dubG9hZC8zMTM2NS8_dG1zdHY9MTczOTUwMzA0Nw==" style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif;color:#333333;text-decoration:none;border-bottom:1px dotted #0875c1;color:#0875c1" url-id="1717342126">please see your PDF gift</a> for a further breakdown of the 7 Cs, and how you can begin using them to unlock your full writing potential, starting today.
</p>
<p style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif;color:#333333;margin-bottom:1em;margin-top:0">And again, welcome to Writers.com.
</p>
<p style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif;color:#333333;margin-bottom:1em;margin-top:0">Warmly,<br style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif;color:#333333">Frederick
</p>
<p style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif;color:#333333;margin-bottom:1em;margin-top:0">p.s. In tomorrow's email, we will be exploring <strong style="font-size:18px;line-height:1.5em;font-family:Georgia, Times, serif;color:#333333">the first C:</strong> Calling—finding the deeper reason you're writing.
</p>
